# Arenibus Marketing Audit — SEO, Performance, Accessibility & Quality

## Project Overview

**Arenibus Marketing Website** is a static Next.js 16 marketing site for the Arenibus nephrology information system (MVP phase).

| Layer | Technology | Location |
|-------|------------|----------|
| Framework | Next.js 16 (App Router, static export) | `app/` |
| Language | TypeScript | `*.ts`, `*.tsx` |
| Styling | Tailwind CSS v4 | `app/globals.css` |
| Assets | Static images, SVG favicon | `public/` |
| Build output | Static HTML/JS/CSS | `out/` |
| Hosting | WebSupport SSH (static files) | `deploy.sh`, `deploy.bat` |
| Primary URL | https://arenibus.polascin.net/ | — |

Key pages:

- `app/page.tsx` — single-page marketing landing page (hero, features, demo CTA, contact)
- `app/layout.tsx` — root layout with metadata and theme provider
- `app/globals.css` — design tokens, light/dark theme CSS variables
- `next.config.ts` — static export configuration
- `public/favicon.svg` — branded SVG favicon
- `public/apple-touch-icon.png` — Apple touch icon

---

## 🎯 Agent Goals

Autonomous agent for deep, recurring audits of the Arenibus marketing site. Covers:

1. **SEO discoverability** — metadata, Open Graph, structured data, sitemap, robots, canonical URLs, heading hierarchy, internal links.
2. **Performance** — Core Web Vitals, bundle size, image/font optimization, static export efficiency.
3. **Accessibility (WCAG 2.1 AA)** — color contrast, keyboard navigation, focus indicators, screen-reader semantics, motion preferences.
4. **Code quality** — TypeScript strictness, Tailwind patterns, Next.js idioms, dead code, dependency hygiene.
5. **Security of static export** — hardcoded secrets, unsafe inline scripts, CSP considerations, dependency vulnerabilities.
6. **Content correctness** — Slovak copy accuracy, brand consistency, demo integration, contact details.
7. **Deployment health** — build succeeds, output is valid, deploy script works, live site matches build.

**Every run ends with a self-improvement step**: record new patterns, false positives, and newly discovered project-specific checks back into this file so the next audit is sharper.

---

## 🔍 Primary Tasks

### 1. SEO Audit

SEO is the primary focus of this audit. The marketing site must rank and share correctly.

#### 1.1 Metadata (`app/layout.tsx`, `app/page.tsx`)

- **Title tag:** unique, descriptive, ≤ 60 chars, includes "Arenibus".
  - Current expected: `Arenibus – Nefrologický a dialyzačný informačný systém`
- **Meta description:** unique per page, ≤ 160 chars, actionable.
- **Canonical URL:** present and points to `https://arenibus.polascin.net/`.
- **Language / locale:** `lang="sk"` or `lang="sk-SK"` set on `<html>`.
- **Open Graph:** `og:title`, `og:description`, `og:type`, `og:url`, `og:image`.
  - `og:image` must be an absolute URL (`https://arenibus.polascin.net/...`) with recommended 1200×630 px.
- **Twitter Cards:** `twitter:card`, `twitter:title`, `twitter:description`, `twitter:image`.
- **Robots meta:** no accidental `noindex` in production.
- **Viewport & theme-color:** present.

#### 1.2 Favicon & PWA basics

- `favicon.ico` or `favicon.svg` linked correctly.
- `apple-touch-icon.png` linked via `<link rel="apple-touch-icon" />`.
- Recommended: `manifest.json` with theme/background colors and icons.
- Theme color matches brand (`#0f766e`).

#### 1.3 Heading hierarchy (`app/page.tsx`)

- Exactly one `<h1>` per page.
- Heading levels must not skip (`h1 → h2 → h3`, never `h1 → h3`).
- Feature section headings use semantic heading tags, not styled `<div>`.
- No empty headings.

#### 1.4 Structured data

- Add `application/ld+json` JSON-LD for `SoftwareApplication` or `Organization`.
  - Required fields: `@context`, `@type`, `name`, `description`, `url`, `applicationCategory`.
  - Optional but recommended: `logo` (square brand mark, not the OG card), `image` (1200×630 social card), `provider`, `featureList`, `softwareVersion`.
  - Do **not** claim `offers.price: "0"` or `isAccessibleForFree: true` while demo access is on request. Do not invent a price. Do not put the password-gated demo URL in `installUrl` or `sameAs`.

#### 1.5 Sitemap & robots

- `public/robots.txt` exists and allows all public paths:
  ```
  User-agent: *
  Allow: /
  Sitemap: https://arenibus.polascin.net/sitemap.xml
  ```
- `public/sitemap.xml` exists and lists the canonical homepage URL with `<lastmod>` and `<priority>`.
- **Note:** Next.js static export does not auto-generate sitemap/robots; they must live in `public/`.

#### 1.6 URL & link hygiene — inventory, verify, fix

Every audit run must **inventory**, **verify**, and **fix** all user-facing web links in the same pass. Do not stop at reporting. Scope is clickable HTTP(S) links, `fetch` endpoints, and `mailto:` addresses.

**Out of scope:** Open Graph, Twitter Cards, `canonical`, JSON-LD `url` / `sameAs`, `metadataBase`, schema.org `@context`.

**Inventory sources** (do not maintain a manual URL allowlist — parse the source):

- `href` on `<a>`, `<Link>`, and `<HashLink>` in `app/**/*.tsx` (`app/page.tsx`, `app/privacy/page.tsx`, `app/terms/page.tsx`, and any future pages). Wrappers that forward `href={prop}` are skipped; the callers are inventoried.
- `fetch("` URLs (contact form → Formspree).
- `Location` headers in `public/privacy.php` and `public/terms.php`.
- `<loc>` entries in `public/sitemap.xml` (treat `https://arenibus.polascin.net/...` as internal paths).

**Internal paths** (`/`, `/privacy/`, `/terms/`): `next.config.ts` sets `trailingSlash: true`. A directory path without a trailing slash is a defect. After `npm run build`, each path must exist as `out/.../index.html` (or the matching static file for extensioned paths such as `.php`).

**Fragments** (`#main-content`, `#features`, `#demo`, `#contact`, and any new ones): the target page must declare a matching `id`.

**External HTTP(S)** (`https://demo.arenibus.com/`, `https://dataprotection.gov.sk/`, Formspree `https://formspree.io/f/mvzebqzj`): GET with a identifiable User-Agent, follow redirects. Success = 2xx/3xx. Formspree GET may return **405** — that is not a failure. Fail on 404, 5xx, timeout, or DNS error. Prefer HTTPS; plain `http://` on a public link is a defect.

**mailto:** only `arenibus@polascin.net` (general contact) and `arenibus@nephroctor.com` (demo-access requests). Query `subject` / `body` is allowed. Any other address is a defect.

**External `<a>` attributes:** `target="_blank"` and `rel` containing both `noopener` and `noreferrer`.

**Anchor text:** descriptive (no "klikni tu" / "viac").

**Fix in the same run:**

- Update a broken `href` to the current official URL; if a stable 301 target exists, point directly at it rather than leaving a redirect chain.
- Add a missing trailing slash or restore a missing `id`.
- Correct a wrong `mailto:` address.
- Add missing `target` / `rel` on external anchors.
- Findings that cannot be fixed safely (e.g. WAF 403 from a CI IP) go under Known Security / project exceptions with the observed status.

**Automated check:** `npm run check:links` (requires a prior `npm run build` so `out/` exists). Must pass locally and in CI.

#### 1.7 Image SEO

- Logo and icons have meaningful `alt` text.
- Decorative images use empty `alt=""`.
- Images are not rendered as text-only `<svg>` without accessible labels.

---

### 2. Performance Audit

- **Core Web Vitals targets:**
  - LCP ≤ 2.5 s
  - INP ≤ 200 ms
  - CLS ≤ 0.1
  - TTFB ≤ 600 ms
- **Bundle:** run `npm run build` and inspect `out/_next/static/...`.
  - No oversized JS chunks.
  - No duplicate icon libraries.
- **Images:**
  - Use Next.js `<Image>` where possible (note: `next/image` with static export may require `unoptimized: true` or loader config).
  - Provide `width`/`height` to prevent CLS.
  - Use WebP/AVIF where appropriate.
- **Fonts:**
  - Self-host or preconnect to font CDN.
  - Use `font-display: swap`.
- **CSS:**
  - Tailwind v4 `@import "tailwindcss"` is correct.
  - No unused custom CSS bloating the bundle.
- **Third-party scripts:** only essential scripts; load async/defer.

---

### 3. Accessibility (WCAG 2.1 AA)

- **Contrast:**
  - Text on brand teal (`--brand: #0f766e`) must meet 4.5:1 for normal text.
  - `--brand-text` (white in light, dark in dark) on brand backgrounds must meet 4.5:1.
  - Links must be distinguishable from body text.
- **Focus:**
  - All interactive elements have visible focus rings.
  - Focus order follows DOM order.
- **Keyboard:**
  - All buttons and links reachable via Tab.
  - Mobile menu toggle works with Enter/Space.
  - Escape closes mobile menu.
- **Screen readers:**
  - Dark mode toggle has `aria-label`.
  - Mobile menu button has `aria-expanded` and `aria-controls`.
  - SVG icons have `aria-hidden` or meaningful labels.
- **Motion:**
  - Respect `prefers-reduced-motion` for any transitions/animations.
- **Language:**
  - `<html lang="sk">` so screen readers use Slovak pronunciation.

---

### 4. Code Quality Audit

#### 4.1 TypeScript

- `strict: true` enabled in `tsconfig.json`.
- No implicit `any`.
- Components have explicit return types or inferred ReactElement.
- No `// @ts-ignore` or `// @ts-nocheck`.

#### 4.2 Next.js / React

- App Router conventions: default exports for pages, correct metadata API.
- No class components.
- Hooks rules respected (no conditional hooks).
- Client components (`"use client"`) only where necessary (theme toggle, localStorage).
- Server Components used by default.

#### 4.3 Tailwind CSS

- Use arbitrary values sparingly; prefer design tokens from `globals.css`.
- No conflicting Tailwind classes on the same element.
- Dark mode uses `data-theme="dark"` and CSS variables, not `dark:` class variants.
- Verify `--color-*` theme registration matches variables used in JSX.

#### 4.4 Dead / unused code

- No commented-out components or imports.
- No unused public assets.
- No dependencies in `package.json` that are not imported.

#### 4.5 Lint

- Run `npm run lint` and fix all errors/warnings.

---

### 5. Security Audit (Static Export)

- **Secrets:** grep for `password`, `secret`, `api_key`, `token`, `private` in code.
  - Must not find hardcoded credentials.
- **Client-side exposure:** no sensitive logic in client components.
- **CSP readiness:** static export cannot set headers; document any inline scripts and recommend moving them to external files.
- **Dependencies:**
  - `npm audit` should pass or have documented acceptable exceptions.
  - Keep Next.js and React up to date.
- **Links:** external links use `rel="noopener noreferrer"`.
- **Forms:** contact form does not submit to insecure endpoint; if backendless, it must clearly indicate that it is informational or use a safe form service.

---

### 6. Content & Brand Consistency Audit

- **Brand alignment:** colors, logo, and tone match `https://demo.arenibus.com/`.
- **Demo section:**
  - Link to `https://demo.arenibus.com/` is correct and reachable (HTTP 2xx/3xx).
  - Demo passwords must never appear in plaintext; access is provided on request via `arenibus@nephroctor.com`.
- **Contact:** `mailto:arenibus@polascin.net` for general contact; `mailto:arenibus@nephroctor.com` for demo-access requests. No other mailto addresses.
- **Slovak copy:**
  - Correct diacritics and grammar.
  - Consistent terminology (e.g., "nefrologická ambulancia", "informačný systém").
- **Feature list:** reflects actual MVP features documented in the core Arenibus system.
- **Copyright / footer:** matches demo site format.

---

### 7. Build & Deployment Audit

- `npm run build` succeeds with zero errors.
- `next.config.ts` sets `output: 'export'` and correct `distDir` or asset prefix if needed.
- `out/` directory contains `index.html` at the root.
- `deploy.sh` / `deploy.bat`:
  - Build step runs before upload.
  - SSH target path is correct: `/data/8/6/868f981d-e598-4e71-b7f5-246f2e180cef/polascin.net/arenibus`.
  - Backup step does not fail silently.
  - Permissions are set after upload.
- **Live verification:** after deploy, `curl -I https://arenibus.polascin.net/` returns `200 OK`.

## 📊 Audit Output Format

For every run, produce a structured report:

```markdown
# Arenibus Marketing Audit Report — <date>

## Summary
- Critical: 0
- High: 0
- Medium: 0
- Low: 0
- Info: 0

## Findings

### <ID>. <Title>
- **Severity:** Critical | High | Medium | Low | Info
- **Category:** SEO | Performance | Accessibility | Code Quality | Security | Content | Deployment
- **File(s):** `<path>`
- **Description:** What is wrong and why it matters.
- **Recommendation:** Specific, actionable fix.

## Fixed in this run
- List of resolved findings.


## Recursive self-improvement (povinné)

Pri každom behu, každej oprave a každej iterácii auditu využívaj **recursive self-improvement** v maximálnej možnej miere:

1. **Po každom kroku vyhodnoť výsledok** — čo funguje, čo nie, čo sa dá spraviť lepšie.
2. **Okamžite vylepši** kód, architektúru, testy, DX, spoľahlivosť a jasnosť, keď vidíš slabé miesto.
3. **Opakuj cyklus** (implementuj → over → pouč sa → vylepši), kým je riešenie robustné, nie len „hotové na prvý pokus“.
4. **Generalizuj poučenia** späť do `.audit.md` / tohto súboru (nové kontroly, falošné poplachy, trivial fixes, vzory chýb).
5. **Nenechávaj polovičaté riešenia** — edge cases a spätnú väzbu z chýb/testov vráť do ďalšej iterácie.
6. **Hranice:** rešpektuj bezpečnostné/MDR/produkčné pravidlá projektu; neoptimalizuj donekonečna bez jasnej hodnoty pre cieľ behu.

## Self-improvement notes
- New patterns to add to `.audit.md` for next runs.
```

Severity definitions:

- **Critical:** broken deploy, build failure, hardcoded secret, security vulnerability, site unavailable.
- **High:** missing canonical/OG/sitemap, severe accessibility blocker, major performance regression.
- **Medium:** heading hierarchy issue, contrast near threshold, missing alt text, dead code.
- **Low:** minor copy inconsistency, missing manifest, suboptimal image format.
- **Info:** suggestions and future improvements.

## 🔄 Self-Improvement Loop

After every audit run:

1. Review false positives and update this file to avoid them next time.
2. Add newly discovered project-specific checks (e.g., a recurring typo, a fragile class pattern, a deployment edge case).
3. Keep the checklist above ordered by business impact: SEO → Performance → Accessibility → Code Quality → Security → Content → Deployment.
4. If a finding repeats across runs, escalate its severity or add an explicit recurring check.

### 2026-07-25 — Add development-phase notice

#### Summary
- Critical: 0
- High: 0
- Medium: 1
- Low: 0

#### Findings

##### 1. Missing development-phase disclaimer
- **Severity:** Medium
- **Category:** Content
- **File(s):** `app/page.tsx`
- **Description:** The marketing page did not visibly state that the information system is still in advanced development. This could create unrealistic expectations for clinical/production use.
- **Recommendation:** Add a clear, prominent notice near the main headline and in the demo section that Arenibus is still in advanced development.
- **Fixed:** Added a badge "Stále vo fáze pokročilého vývoja" under the hero `<h1>` and updated the demo section text to say "Funkčné demo nefrologickej ambulancie v pokročilej fáze vývoja."

#### Fixed in this run
- Added development-phase badge in hero section.
- Updated demo section wording to reflect advanced development status.

#### Self-improvement notes
- Keep the development-phase badge visible until the system leaves MVP/advanced-development status.
- Revisit the disclaimer before any production-ready release.

#### Summary
- Critical: 0

### 2026-07-24 — Automated deep audit

#### Summary
- Critical: 0
- High: 0
- Medium: 0
- Low: 0
- Info: 1

#### Findings

##### 1. Meta description exceeded 160 characters
- **Severity:** Medium
- **Category:** SEO
- **File(s):** `app/layout.tsx`
- **Description:** The `<meta name="description">` content was 197 characters long, which may be truncated in search-engine snippets.
- **Recommendation:** Shorten the description to ≤ 160 characters while keeping the key value proposition.
- **Fixed:** Shortened to 102 characters: "Moderný .NET systém pre nefrologickú ambulanciu a dialyzačné stredisko, integrovaný s ezdravotníctvom."

##### 2. ARIA labels on interactive controls used English copy
- **Severity:** Low
- **Category:** Accessibility
- **File(s):** `app/page.tsx`
- **Description:** The dark-mode toggle used `aria-label="Toggle dark mode"` and the mobile menu used `aria-label="Menu"`, while the rest of the interface is in Slovak.
- **Recommendation:** Localize all screen-reader labels to Slovak.
- **Fixed:** Updated to `aria-label="Prepnúť tmavý režim"` and `aria-label="Otvoriť alebo zavrieť menu"`.

##### 3. Sitemap `lastmod` was stale
- **Severity:** Low
- **Category:** SEO
- **File(s):** `public/sitemap.xml`
- **Description:** The `<lastmod>` value was `2026-07-22`, two days behind the current deploy date.
- **Recommendation:** Update `<lastmod>` on each deploy or automate it via the deploy pipeline.
- **Fixed:** Updated to `2026-07-24`.

##### 4. Known transitive dependency vulnerabilities
- **Severity:** Info
- **Category:** Security
- **File(s):** `package-lock.json`
- **Description:** `npm audit` reports 3 high-severity issues in Next.js 16.2.11 transitive dependencies (`postcss`, `sharp`). The suggested fix downgrades Next.js to 9.3.3, which is a breaking change.
- **Recommendation:** Monitor Next.js releases and upgrade when a patched 16.x version is available. This is already documented as a known exception.

#### Fixed in this run
- Shortened meta description in `app/layout.tsx`.
- Localized dark-mode and mobile-menu `aria-label` values in `app/page.tsx`.
- Updated `public/sitemap.xml` `<lastmod>`.
- Verified build, lint, contrast ratios, heading hierarchy, alt text, canonical/OG/Twitter tags, JSON-LD, robots.txt, and manifest.

#### Self-improvement notes
- Add a recurring check that the meta description stays ≤ 160 characters.
- Add a recurring check that all `aria-label` text on the page is in Slovak.
- Consider automating `<lastmod>` in `sitemap.xml` via the CI/CD workflow.
- Contrast ratios for the current palette are well above WCAG 2.1 AA thresholds; no extra tooling needed unless colors change.

- **Recommendation:** Run `npm audit fix` to update within the existing semver range.
- **Fixed:** Ran `npm audit fix`, which updated `nanoid` to `3.3.18` (or later) without touching `package.json` ranges. Verified with `npm run lint` and `npm run build`.

##### 3. Known transitive dependency vulnerabilities remain (postcss/sharp via Next.js)
- **Severity:** Info
- **Category:** Security
- **File(s):** `package-lock.json`
- **Description:** `npm audit --omit=dev` still reports high-severity transitive vulnerabilities in `postcss` and `sharp` through Next.js 16.2.11. The only remediation path upgrades Next.js to `16.3.1`, published 2026-08-13 — only 2 days before this audit, short of the project's 7-day vetting window.
- **Recommendation:** Re-evaluate upgrading to Next.js 16.3.1 (or newer) on or after 2026-08-20, then run the full audit and regression checks before adopting it.

#### Fixed in this run
- Updated `public/sitemap.xml` `<lastmod>`.
- Ran `npm audit fix` to resolve the `nanoid` vulnerability without breaking changes.
- Verified lint, production build, `npm audit` (remaining findings documented above), live homepage, sitemap.xml, demo availability, and page content/copy.

#### Self-improvement notes
- Track Next.js release dates before upgrading; do not adopt a release younger than 7 days per project policy.
- `npm audit fix` (without `--force`) is safe to run each audit round to pick up in-range transitive patches; only `--force` requires manual review.
- Sitemap `<lastmod>` drifts noticeably between infrequent audit runs; consider wiring it to the deploy workflow's date at build time.

### 2026-08-28 — Automated deep audit

#### Summary
- Critical: 1
- High: 3
- Medium: 5
- Low: 8
- Info: 3

#### Findings

##### 1. `deploy.sh` could wipe the wrong directory
- **Severity:** Critical
- **Category:** Deployment
- **File(s):** `deploy.sh`
- **Description:** The remote block ran `cd $DEPLOY_DIR` with no failure guard and then `rm -rf *`. If the deploy directory were ever renamed, unmounted or unreachable, `cd` would fail, the script would continue in the SSH login home directory, and `rm -rf *` would delete the home contents. The GitHub Actions workflow already had `cd ... || exit 1`; the manual fallback script did not.
- **Recommendation:** Guard the `cd`, add `set -euo pipefail`, and replace the bare glob with a bounded `find`.
- **Fixed:** Rewrote `deploy.sh` with `set -euo pipefail`, an explicit `if ! cd "$DEPLOY_DIR"; then ... exit 1; fi` guard, quoted variables, and `find . -mindepth 1 -maxdepth 1 -exec rm -rf -- {} +`.

##### 2. Hero LCP image was a 4.73 MB PNG
- **Severity:** High
- **Category:** Performance
- **File(s):** `public/ArenibusLogo.png`, `app/page.tsx`
- **Description:** A single 2048x2048, 4.73 MB PNG served as the hero image (rendered at 384 CSS px, marked `priority`), the nav logo (40 px), the Open Graph image, the Apple touch icon and a manifest icon. With `images.unoptimized: true` (required by `output: 'export'`), Next.js serves the file byte-for-byte, so every visitor downloaded 4.73 MB for the LCP element. It accounted for 4.84 MB of the 5.9 MB build output — 82 % of the payload.
- **Recommendation:** Generate purpose-sized derivatives; the source is photographic/illustrative, so WebP compresses it dramatically.
- **Fixed:** Generated `logo-768.webp` (61 KB, hero) and `logo-96.webp` (3.2 KB, nav) with sharp, and repointed both `<Image>` elements. Hero image payload dropped 98.7 % (4842 KB to 61 KB); total `out/` dropped from 5.9 MB to 1.37 MB (− 77 %). After rebasing onto the privacy/terms pages added in 54064b4, the full six-route output is 1.53 MB.

##### 3. Deployment backup was destroyed immediately after creation
- **Severity:** High
- **Category:** Deployment
- **File(s):** `.github/workflows/deploy.yml`, `deploy.sh`
- **Description:** Both deploy paths ran `tar -czf "backup_$(date ...).tar.gz" .` **inside** `$DEPLOY_DIR` and then wiped the directory. The cleanup glob matched the archive that had just been written, so every deployment silently destroyed its own rollback point. The archive also tried to include itself while being written.
- **Recommendation:** Write the archive outside the deploy directory and retain a bounded history.
- **Fixed:** Backups now go to `$HOME/arenibus-backups/backup_<timestamp>.tar.gz` via `tar -czf "$BACKUP_FILE" -C "$DEPLOY_DIR" .`, keeping the five most recent archives and pruning older ones. Applied to both the workflow and `deploy.sh`. The first deploy confirmed the archive is now written to `/data/8/6/<uuid>/arenibus-backups/`, outside the wiped tree.
- **Follow-up:** The first run reported `Backup size: 512`, which read as a suspiciously empty archive. Rather than trust or dismiss it, the step was made to prove its own output: it counts entries under `$DEPLOY_DIR` before archiving, re-reads the archive with `tar -tzf`, prints exact byte and entry counts, and aborts *before* the cleanup if the archive holds fewer entries than the source. The next deploy reported `Backup: 621955 bytes, 60 entries` against 59 source entries (the extra one is the archive root `.`), confirming the backup was genuine all along and that `du -h` had simply been reporting 512-byte allocation blocks. The verification stays in place so the question can never be ambiguous again.

##### 4. Three high-severity dependency vulnerabilities
- **Severity:** High
- **Category:** Security
- **File(s):** `package.json`, `package-lock.json`
- **Description:** `npm audit` reported three high-severity advisories reachable through Next.js 16.2.11: four `postcss` issues (XSS via unescaped `</style>`, plus three `sourceMappingURL` arbitrary-file-read / path-traversal advisories) and `sharp <0.35.0` inheriting four libvips CVEs.
- **Recommendation:** Upgrade Next.js rather than using the breaking `npm audit fix --force` suggestion (which proposed `next@9.3.3` in an earlier run).
- **Fixed:** Upgraded to `next@16.3.1` and `eslint-config-next@16.3.1` (pinned exactly). 16.3.1 ships `postcss@8.5.23` and `sharp@^0.35.3`, clearing all three advisories. Chosen over the newer 16.3.2 (5 days old) and 16.3.3 (1 day old) because only 16.3.1 (13 days old) satisfied the project's 7-day vetting window. `npm audit` now reports **0 vulnerabilities**.

##### 5. Open Graph image had the wrong aspect ratio and size
- **Severity:** Medium
- **Category:** SEO
- **File(s):** `app/layout.tsx`, `public/og-image.png`
- **Description:** `og:image` pointed at the square 2048x2048 logo while declaring `width: 1200, height: 630`. Social platforms trust the declared dimensions, so link previews were letterboxed or cropped, and the 4.73 MB payload exceeds several platforms' fetch limits.
- **Recommendation:** Ship a real 1200x630 card and keep the declared dimensions truthful.
- **Fixed:** Composited a dedicated `og-image.png` (1200x630, 139 KB) — the logo centred on the background colour sampled from its own border (`#1e374a`) for a seamless card. `og:image` and `twitter:image` now point to it; verified the absolute URL is emitted in `out/index.html`.

##### 6. Development badge failed WCAG 2.1 AA contrast (light mode)
- **Severity:** Medium
- **Category:** Accessibility
- **File(s):** `app/globals.css`
- **Description:** The "Stále vo fáze pokročilého vývoja" badge renders `text-sm` in `--warn` on `--warn-soft`. Measured contrast was **4.31:1**, below the 4.5:1 AA threshold for normal text. Earlier rounds recorded the palette as "well above WCAG 2.1 AA thresholds" without ever measuring it.
- **Recommendation:** Darken `--warn` minimally, keeping the amber hue.
- **Fixed:** `--warn: #b45309` to `#ae5009`, giving **4.57:1**.

##### 7. Footer secondary text failed WCAG 2.1 AA contrast (dark mode)
- **Severity:** Medium
- **Category:** Accessibility
- **File(s):** `app/globals.css`
- **Description:** The footer renders `text-sm text-muted` on `bg-surface-3`. In dark mode `--muted` on `--surface-3` measured **4.43:1**, below the 4.5:1 AA threshold.
- **Recommendation:** Lighten `--muted` minimally in both dark blocks.
- **Fixed:** `--muted: #8fa8a0` to `#92aba3` in the `[data-theme="dark"]` block *and* the `prefers-color-scheme: dark` block, giving **4.59:1**.

##### 8. Horizontal overflow on narrow viewports
- **Severity:** Medium
- **Category:** Accessibility
- **File(s):** `app/page.tsx`
- **Description:** The hero logo wrapper used a fixed `w-96 h-96` (384 px). On a 360 px viewport the section's `px-4` padding leaves 328 px, so the wrapper forced the document 12 px wider than the viewport, producing horizontal scrolling on common phone widths — a Google mobile-friendliness signal.
- **Recommendation:** Let the wrapper shrink below its maximum size.
- **Fixed:** `w-96 h-96` to `w-full max-w-96 aspect-square`. Measured in-browser at a 360 px viewport: `scrollWidth` 372 to 360, overflowing elements 8 to 0. The existing `sizes="(max-width: 768px) 100vw, 384px"` is now accurate.

##### 9. Apple touch icon served the full-size logo
- **Severity:** Medium
- **Category:** Performance / SEO
- **File(s):** `app/layout.tsx`, `public/apple-touch-icon.png`
- **Description:** `icons.apple` pointed at the 4.73 MB logo, so iOS downloaded the whole file to render a 180x180 home-screen icon.
- **Fixed:** Added `apple-touch-icon.png` (180x180, 21 KB) and repointed the metadata.

##### 10. Manifest declared incorrect icon dimensions
- **Severity:** Low
- **Category:** SEO / PWA
- **File(s):** `public/manifest.json`
- **Description:** The manifest declared `/ArenibusLogo.png` as `sizes: "512x512"` while the file was 2048x2048, and listed `/favicon.ico` as a PWA icon. Mismatched `sizes` makes installability checks unreliable.
- **Fixed:** Replaced with truthful `icon-192.png` (192x192) and `icon-512.png` (512x512) entries plus `purpose: "any"`, and added `lang: "sk-SK"` and `scope: "/"`.

##### 11. Dead assets shipped in `public/`
- **Severity:** Low
- **Category:** Code Quality
- **File(s):** `public/favicon.ico`, `public/ArenibusLogo.png`
- **Description:** `public/favicon.ico` (168 KB) was shadowed by the App Router file convention `app/favicon.ico` (25 KB) — confirmed because `out/favicon.ico` always matched the `app/` copy byte-for-byte — so it was never served. After the image work, `ArenibusLogo.png` had no remaining references.
- **Fixed:** Removed both. The 2048x2048 master remains recoverable from git history (`git show c0af678:public/ArenibusLogo.png`). Verified no dangling references in `out/`, that `/ArenibusLogo.png` now 404s, and that `/favicon.ico` still serves the `app/` icon.

##### 12. Theme flash on load for users overriding the system preference
- **Severity:** Low
- **Category:** Accessibility / Performance
- **File(s):** `app/layout.tsx`
- **Description:** `data-theme` was only applied in a `useEffect` after hydration. The CSS `prefers-color-scheme` fallback covered users whose stored choice matched their system, but anyone who had toggled against it saw a flash of the wrong theme on every load.
- **Fixed independently on `main`.** This round produced its own blocking `themeInit` script, but commit 54064b4 had already landed an equivalent bootstrap (plus `suppressHydrationWarning`). During the rebase the duplicate was removed and the `main` version kept: it sets `data-theme` only when `localStorage` holds a choice, leaving the CSS `prefers-color-scheme` block to serve users who never toggled. Recorded because the finding was reached independently and the surviving single script is the intended end state.

##### 13. Mobile menu stayed open after choosing a link
- **Severity:** Low
- **Category:** Accessibility
- **File(s):** `app/page.tsx`
- **Description:** Selecting a section link in the mobile menu scrolled the page but left the menu overlaying the content.
- **Fixed:** Each mobile link now calls `setMobileMenuOpen(false)`.

##### 14. Form feedback live region was created on demand
- **Severity:** Low
- **Category:** Accessibility
- **File(s):** `app/page.tsx`
- **Description:** The success/error messages mounted their `role="status"` / `role="alert"` element at the same moment as their text. Screen readers can miss announcements when the live region and its content appear in the same update.
- **Fixed:** Replaced with a single persistent `role="status" aria-live="polite"` element that is `sr-only` while idle and gains the success/error styling when a message appears.

##### 15. Sitemap `lastmod` was stale
- **Severity:** Low
- **Category:** SEO
- **File(s):** `public/sitemap.xml`
- **Description:** Listed `2026-08-15`, 13 days behind this release.
- **Fixed:** Updated to `2026-08-28`.

##### 16. `.trunk/` polluted the working tree
- **Severity:** Low
- **Category:** Code Quality
- **File(s):** `.gitignore`
- **Description:** An untracked `.trunk/` directory from a local trunk.io run appeared in every `git status`.
- **Fixed:** Added `.trunk/` to `.gitignore`. Note this also ignores `trunk.yaml` and `configs/`, which trunk.io normally expects to be committed — deliberately left uncommitted, because adopting trunk.io as project tooling is a maintainer decision, not an audit change.

##### 17. Deploy permission handling was imprecise
- **Severity:** Low
- **Category:** Deployment
- **File(s):** `.github/workflows/deploy.yml`, `deploy.sh`
- **Description:** `chmod -R 755 .` followed by `chmod -R 644 *.html *.json *.js *.css` relied on top-level globs and would have stripped the execute bit from directories had any glob matched one.
- **Fixed:** Replaced with `find . -type d -exec chmod 755 {} +` and `find . -type f -exec chmod 644 {} +`.

##### 18. `"Inter Variable"` is referenced but never loaded
- **Severity:** Info
- **Category:** Performance / Content
- **File(s):** `app/globals.css`
- **Description:** The body font stack starts with `"Inter Variable"`, but there is no `@font-face`, `next/font` usage or CDN link anywhere. Visitors who happen to have Inter installed locally see Inter; everyone else falls back to Segoe UI / `system-ui`, so typography differs between visitors.
- **Recommendation:** Either self-host Inter via `next/font/local` for consistency (costs a font download) or drop the name for a purely system-font stack (keeps the current zero-download behaviour). **Deliberately not changed** — it alters the site's typography and is a design decision for the maintainer.

##### 19. Local `npm run build` is blocked by Windows Application Control
- **Severity:** Info
- **Category:** Deployment
- **Description:** On this workstation the default Turbopack build fails: a WDAC policy blocks `@next/swc-win32-x64-msvc/next-swc.win32-x64-msvc.node`, Next.js falls back to WASM bindings, and Turbopack refuses to run without native bindings. CI is unaffected (ubuntu-latest has working native bindings).
- **Recommendation:** Use `npx next build --webpack` for local verification. `npm run build` was **left on Turbopack** so CI keeps the faster path; this is a workstation policy issue, not a repository defect.

##### 20. CSP readiness unchanged by the new inline script
- **Severity:** Info
- **Category:** Security
- **File(s):** `app/layout.tsx`
- **Description:** `out/index.html` contains three bare inline `<script>` blocks: the new `themeInit` plus two Next.js RSC payload scripts (`self.__next_f.push(...)`), alongside the `application/ld+json` block. A future CSP would need hashes or `'unsafe-inline'` regardless of the theme script, so it does not change the existing posture. Static export cannot set headers, so any CSP must come from the hosting layer.

#### Fixed in this run
- Rewrote `deploy.sh` safely (`set -euo pipefail`, guarded `cd`, bounded `find`-based cleanup, quoted variables, post-deploy HTTP verification).
- Moved deployment backups outside the wiped directory with 5-archive retention, in both the workflow and `deploy.sh`.
- Replaced the 4.73 MB logo with purpose-built assets: `logo-768.webp`, `logo-96.webp`, `og-image.png`, `apple-touch-icon.png`, `icon-192.png`, `icon-512.png`.
- Upgraded to Next.js 16.3.1 — `npm audit` now reports 0 vulnerabilities.
- Fixed two measured WCAG 2.1 AA contrast failures and the mobile horizontal overflow.
- Added mobile-menu close-on-select and a persistent form live region. (The pre-paint theme script was already fixed on `main`; the duplicate introduced this round was dropped during the rebase.)
- Corrected `manifest.json`, refreshed `sitemap.xml`, removed two dead assets, ignored `.trunk/`.
- Added `scripts/check-contrast.mjs` plus `npm run check:contrast`, wired into CI as a required step.

#### Verification performed
- `npm run lint` clean; `npx next build --webpack` succeeds for all six routes (`/`, `/privacy`, `/terms`, `/_not-found`); `npm audit` reports 0 vulnerabilities. Every check was re-run after rebasing onto 54064b4.
- `npm run check:contrast`: all 26 token pairs pass in both themes, plus dark-palette parity between the two dark CSS blocks.
- Served `out/` locally and confirmed HTTP 200 and correct `Content-Type` for every asset, and that `/ArenibusLogo.png` now 404s.
- Structural checks on the generated HTML: exactly one `<h1>`, no skipped heading levels, all four fragment anchors resolve, external links carry `rel="noopener noreferrer"`, both images have `alt`, all 9 SVGs are `aria-hidden`, all three form fields have `<label for>`, all `aria-label`s are Slovak.
- Headless-Chrome renders at 1280 px (light and dark) and 360 px; in-browser layout measurement confirmed zero overflowing elements after the fix.
- Live checks: `https://arenibus.polascin.net/`, `/robots.txt`, `/sitemap.xml` and `https://demo.arenibus.com/` all returned HTTP 200.

#### Self-improvement notes
- **Measure, do not assert.** Two real AA contrast failures (4.31:1 and 4.43:1) survived several rounds because a previous run recorded the palette as "well above WCAG thresholds" without measuring. `npm run check:contrast` now runs in CI so this cannot regress silently.
- **Do not trust headless screenshots for layout facts.** The 360 px screenshot appeared to show nav controls pushed off-screen and content severely clipped; direct `getBoundingClientRect` measurement showed the real defect was a 12 px overflow. Use screenshots to spot *candidates*, then confirm geometry by measuring inside the page.
- **Audit asset weight, not just asset presence.** Earlier rounds confirmed that `og:image`, the apple icon and manifest icons *existed*, but never checked what they weighed or whether declared dimensions matched the file. One 4.73 MB file was doing five different jobs badly.
- **Purged Tailwind classes invalidate before/after DOM experiments.** Re-applying an old utility class (`w-96`) in the browser did nothing, because the class no longer exists in the built CSS. Reproduce prior geometry with inline styles instead.
- **A backup is not fixed until it is verified.** Moving the archive out of the wiped directory was necessary but not sufficient, and a `du -h` figure of "512" was not evidence either way. Deploy steps that guard against data loss should assert their own output in unambiguous units (bytes and entry counts), not just exit 0.
- **Check the manual deploy path, not just CI.**
- Next.js 16.3.2 (2026-08-21) and 16.3.3 (2026-08-25) were available but below the 7-day vetting window at audit time; re-evaluate next run.
- Demo credentials are no longer published on the site at all: 54064b4 replaced them with an e-mail request flow, and the standing check is now that they must never appear in plain text in this public repository. The earlier "cross-check against the main repo README" check is retired.
- Two branches independently produced a theme-flash fix in the same release. Before adding a bootstrap script to `<head>`, check whether `main` already ships one.

Production is a Linux static export, but local `next dev` / `next build` runs on a Windows workstation. The 7-day feature-vetting window does not apply to critical security patches.

#### Self-improvement notes
- Measure meta description length in **every** generated route (`out/index.html`, `out/privacy/index.html`, `out/terms/index.html`), not only the homepage.

- **Recommendation:** `npm audit fix` without `--force`.
- **Fixed:** `fast-uri` is now 3.1.7. `npm audit` reports 0 vulnerabilities. `serve` is a devDependency used only by `npm start`.

- **Severity:** Info

The static HTML footer on the demo landing still reads `Arenibus v 0.17.41` until client hydration; the JS constant remains the source of truth per standing checks.
- **Recommendation:** Refresh the single `DEMO_VERSION` constant whenever the demo bundle constant changes.
- **Fixed:** Both badges now read `v0.17.42` via `DEMO_VERSION`.

##### 2. Demo username `<code>` used hardcoded white text
- **Severity:** Low
- **Category:** Accessibility
- **File(s):** `app/page.tsx`
- **Description:** `demo-lekar` / `demo-sestra` codes used `text-white` on the brand teal band. In dark mode `--brand` is `#2dd4bf`, and white-on-teal fails WCAG 2.1 AA (same class of defect as the earlier `hover:text-white` finding).
- **Recommendation:** Use `text-brand-text` for any text on the demo brand band; never introduce raw `text-white` there.
- **Fixed:** Both `<code>` elements now use `text-brand-text`.

##### 3. Mobile menu `aria-controls` pointed at a missing node when closed
- **Severity:** Low
- **Category:** Accessibility
- **File(s):** `app/page.tsx`
- **Description:** The menu was mounted only when `mobileMenuOpen` was true, so `aria-controls="mobile-menu"` referenced a non-existent `id` while the menu was closed.
- **Recommendation:** Keep `#mobile-menu` in the DOM and toggle visibility with `hidden` / CSS.
- **Fixed:** Menu container is always rendered; closed state uses the `hidden` attribute and `hidden` class.

##### 4. Next.js 16.3.4 remains inside the 7-day feature-vetting window
- **Severity:** Info
- **Category:** Security / Code Quality
- **Description:** Latest stable is still `next@16.3.4` (2026-08-31). No newer 16.3.x security release. Four days old as of this run; re-evaluate on or after 2026-09-07. `npm audit` reports 0 vulnerabilities on `next@16.3.3`.

##### 5. Largest JS chunk baseline (Turbopack vs local webpack)
- **Severity:** Info
- **Category:** Performance
- **Description:** Local `npx next build --webpack` emits a ~241 KB chunk; CI Turbopack (`npm run build`) previously recorded ~229 KB for the homepage client bundle. No new client libraries. Treat Turbopack CI output as the production baseline.

##### 2. Legal pages had no skip-to-content target (WCAG 2.4.1)
- **Severity:** Medium
- **Category:** Accessibility
- **File(s):** `app/privacy/page.tsx`, `app/terms/page.tsx`
- **Description:** `/privacy/` and `/terms/` have a sticky nav but no bypass link. The homepage already offered „Preskočiť na hlavný obsah“ → `#main-content`. Keyboard users landing on the legal pages had to tab through the logo and back-link before reaching the article.
- **Recommendation:** Add the same skip link and `id="main-content"` on `<main>` of every route.
- **Fixed:** Both legal pages now match the homepage skip pattern. Keep the markup inline — `npm run check:links` resolves fragments against the file that declares the `href`, so a shared skip component fails the check.

##### 3. Dark-mode toggle did not expose pressed state (WCAG 4.1.2)
- **Severity:** Medium
- **Category:** Accessibility
- **File(s):** `app/page.tsx`
- **Description:** The theme button had a Slovak `aria-label` but no `aria-pressed`. Assistive tech could not tell whether dark mode was currently on.
- **Recommendation:** Set `aria-pressed={darkMode}` on the toggle.
- **Fixed:** The control now emits `aria-pressed="false"` (default / light) and updates with the stored preference.

##### 4. JSON-LD omitted the live demo version and logo
- **Severity:** Low
- **Category:** SEO
- **File(s):** `app/layout.tsx`, `lib/site.ts`
- **Description:** `SoftwareApplication` structured data had name, description, URL, category and offers, but not `softwareVersion`, `inLanguage`, or `logo`. The version lived only in the client homepage badges, so a crawler could not see that the advertised release is 0.17.107.
- **Recommendation:** Drive JSON-LD `softwareVersion` from the same constant as the badges.
- **Fixed:** Shared `DEMO_VERSION` / `DEMO_VERSION_PLAIN` in `lib/site.ts`; JSON-LD now includes `softwareVersion: "0.17.107"`, `inLanguage: "sk"`, and `logo` pointing at the 1200×630 OG image.

##### 2. Demo-access card and OIDC caption failed WCAG 2.1 AA in light mode
- **Severity:** Medium
- **Category:** Accessibility
- **File(s):** `app/page.tsx`, `scripts/check-contrast.mjs`
- **Description:** Small text on the teal demo band used translucent overlays (`bg-surface/10`, inner `bg-surface/20`) plus `text-brand-text/90` and nested `opacity-75`. Effective contrast in light mode dropped to ~2.6:1 for labels and ~3.3:1 for 90 % white on the frosted card — below the 4.5:1 AA floor for `text-xs` / `text-sm`. The OIDC caption used `text-brand-text/80` (4.13:1 on `--brand`). The token checker only sampled solid `brand-text` on `--brand`, so the failure was invisible to CI.
- **Recommendation:** Put small text on opaque `surface` / `surface-2` cards. Do not combine Tailwind opacity modifiers with `text-brand-text` on the demo gradient.
- **Fixed:** Demo-access block is a solid `bg-surface` card with `foreground` / `foreground-2` copy and a brand primary CTA. OIDC caption is full-opacity `text-brand-text`. Contrast checker now includes version/role badge, brand-on-surface, username code, theme-toggle icon, and OIDC-on-brand-strong pairs.

##### 3. Legal pages had no theme toggle
- **Severity:** Medium
- **Category:** Accessibility
- **File(s):** `app/theme-toggle.tsx`, `app/page.tsx`, `app/privacy/page.tsx`, `app/terms/page.tsx`
- **Description:** Root layout applies `data-theme` from `localStorage` on every route, so `/privacy/` and `/terms/` rendered in dark mode after a homepage visit, but those sticky navs had no control to switch back. Keyboard and screen-reader users could not change the pressed state of the theme except on `/`.
- **Recommendation:** Keep one shared client `ThemeToggle` (`aria-label` + `aria-pressed`) on every sticky nav. Leave skip links inline.
- **Fixed:** Extracted `app/theme-toggle.tsx` and mounted it on `/`, `/privacy/`, and `/terms/`.

- Do not use nested Tailwind opacity (`text-brand-text/90` plus `opacity-75`) on the demo band.
- Theme toggle must appear on every sticky nav (`/`, `/privacy/`, `/terms/`), not only the homepage, because `themeInit` in the root layout applies `data-theme` globally.
- `next@16.3.5` is still inside the feature-vetting window (re-evaluate 2026-09-18 or on a GHSA).

##### 1. Live demo is still v0.17.157 — no new clinician tiles

- **Fixed:** Version constant left at `v0.17.157`. Feature-card copy left aligned with Run 23/24.

##### 2. Contact form failed closed when Formspree was blocked
- **Severity:** Medium
- **Category:** Accessibility / Security
- **File(s):** `app/contact-form.tsx`
- **Description:** Parent OpenResty CSP still has `connect-src` without `formspree.io` and `form-action 'self'`. A native POST to Formspree would be blocked; `fetch` fails in the browser. The previous error copy mentioned e-mail only as prose. No-JS users had no in-form path.
- **Recommendation:** Keep trying Formspree (works if the parent CSP is later relaxed). On failure, expose a real `mailto:arenibus@polascin.net` with a prefilled subject/body. Add `<noscript>` with the same address. Do not set `action="https://formspree.io/..."` — `form-action 'self'` would block it.
- **Fixed:** Mailto fallback + noscript + `maxLength` + Formspree `_gotcha` honeypot. Visible error link stays a static `mailto:` so `check:links` can resolve it.

##### 3. Missing branded 404
- **Severity:** Medium
- **Category:** SEO / Accessibility
- **File(s):** `app/not-found.tsx`
- **Description:** Static export only had the default Next.js not-found page. A broken inbound link showed no skip link, theme toggle, or Slovak chrome.
- **Fixed:** `app/not-found.tsx` with inline skip link, sticky nav, theme toggle, and shared footer. Next.js still emits `noindex` on `/_not-found/` (correct).

##### 4. Legal pages had no breadcrumbs or `aria-current`
- **Severity:** Low
- **Category:** SEO / Accessibility
- **File(s):** `app/breadcrumbs.tsx`, `app/privacy/page.tsx`, `app/terms/page.tsx`, `app/site-footer.tsx`
- **Description:** `/privacy/` and `/terms/` had no visible path back to the homepage besides the nav label, no `BreadcrumbList` JSON-LD, and footer links did not mark the current page.
- **Fixed:** Visible breadcrumbs, `BreadcrumbList` JSON-LD, shared `SiteFooter` with `aria-current="page"`.

##### 5. Theme toggle ignored OS preference changes
- **Severity:** Low
- **Category:** Accessibility
- **File(s):** `app/theme-toggle.tsx`
- **Description:** `useSyncExternalStore` only subscribed to `storage` events. With no saved `localStorage` value, a system theme change did not update `aria-pressed` or `data-theme`.
- **Fixed:** Also subscribe to `matchMedia("(prefers-color-scheme: dark)")` when no stored preference exists.

##### 6. Homepage brand mark was not a link; mobile menu icon did not reflect open state
- **Severity:** Low
- **Category:** Accessibility
- **File(s):** `app/site-header.tsx`
- **Fixed:** Logo + name now link to `/`. The menu SVG switches to an X while `aria-expanded` is true.

- **Category:** SEO / Security
- **File(s):** `public/.well-known/security.txt`, `app/layout.tsx`, `app/privacy/page.tsx`
- **Description:** No RFC 9116 contact file. IČO `57646856` can be auto-linked as a phone number on iOS. JSON-LD had `SoftwareApplication` but no `WebSite` node and no `installUrl` for the demo.
- **Fixed:** `security.txt` (expires 2027-03-20, linked from the privacy page), `formatDetection.telephone: false`, `applicationName`, `WebSite` JSON-LD, `installUrl` on the software node. Legal OG image alts now match the homepage.

- `npm audit` / `npm audit --omit=dev`: 0 vulnerabilities.

- Generated HTML: homepage title 54 / description 137; privacy 41 / 150; terms 31 / 121; `lang="sk"`; canonicals absolute; `og:image` absolute; no `noindex` on indexable routes (`/_not-found/` correctly has `noindex`); exactly one `<h1>` per route; version `v0.17.157` on the homepage; JSON-LD `softwareVersion` `0.17.157`, `featureList`, `WebSite`, and legal `BreadcrumbList`; skip link + `<main id="main-content">` on `/`, `/privacy/`, `/terms/`, `/_not-found/`; theme toggle `aria-pressed`; visible contact labels; `format-detection` telephone=no; `application-name`.

#### Self-improvement notes
- When demo HTML `Last-Modified` and `/assets/index-*.js` hash are unchanged, do not invent feature tiles.
- Parent CSP `form-action 'self'` and `connect-src` without `formspree.io` mean the contact form must keep a `mailto:` fallback. Never add a Formspree `action` attribute.
- `app/not-found.tsx` needs the same inline skip link rule as the three public routes.
- `check:links` must resolve `fetch(IDENT)` via file-level `const` strings and inventory `<form action>`.
- Keep `public/security.txt` (and a copy under `public/.well-known/`) with an `Expires` date less than one year out. The privacy-page and footer link must be `/security/` — WebSupport OpenResty 404s `/.well-known/` and WAF 403s `/security.txt`. Set `Canonical` in the text file to the HTML `/security/` page. Parent ErrorDocument is also not our `/_not-found/` page.
- Shared chrome (footer) is fine; skip links stay inline on each page.

- **Severity:** Low
- **Category:** Accessibility
- **File(s):** `app/contact-form.tsx`
- **Description:** `_gotcha` used `className="hidden"` and `aria-hidden` but not the HTML `hidden` attribute, so it remained a named form control in the accessibility tree if CSS failed to load.

### Run 33 — 2026-10-02 (Demo privacy scope — resolved)

#### Summary
- Critical: 0
- High: 1 (fixed)
- Medium: 0
- Low: 0

#### Findings

##### 1. The demo linked to a privacy policy that explicitly excluded the demo
- **Severity:** High
- **Category:** Content / Legal
- **File(s):** `app/privacy/page.tsx`
- **Description:** The demo login screen and the demo landing footer both link „Ochrana osobných
  údajov“ to `https://arenibus.polascin.net/privacy/` (hardcoded in `frontend/src/App.tsx` of
  `arenibus-nis`). § 6 of that document said the demo runs „s vlastným spracúvaním údajov, na ktoré
  sa tieto zásady nevzťahujú“ — so the link led to a page that disclaimed the very environment it
  was linked from. The conflict was confirmed on 2026-10-01 and again on 2026-10-02.
- **Recommendation:** Extend the existing policy with a clearly separated demo scope rather than
  publishing a second document — the demo's link target is compiled into its frontend build, so a
  new URL would stay dead until the demo is rebuilt.
- **Fixed:** Added a „Rozsah týchto zásad“ box up front (articles 1–6 = marketing site, article 7 =
  demo), rescoped the § 2 and § 5 headings to the website, replaced the § 6 exclusion sentence with
  a pointer to article 7, and added **article 7 „Demo prostredie (demo.arenibus.com)“** with 7.1
  prihlasovanie, 7.2 browser storage, 7.3 logy a audit, 7.4 hosting a príjemcovia, 7.5 prenosy do
  tretích krajín, 7.6 nočná obnova a doba uchovávania, 7.7 čo do dema nezadávajte. Former articles
  7 and 8 renumbered to 8 and 9; effective date moved to 2026-10-02; `id="demo"` anchor added and
  linked twice.

#### Demo configuration established for article 7
Source of truth: local demo repo `D:/Dev/arenibus` @ `bb0fdf4` (the commit deployed to demo on
2026-10-01), cross-checked against the live instance.

| Aspect | Finding | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Version | Live bundle `index-BVTS1dQ0.js` carries `Mp="0.17.173"` — matches the deployed commit | live fetch |
| Login | Keycloak 26.3 on the same domain under `/auth`, realm `arenibus`, OIDC Authorization Code + PKCE (no external OIDC library). Demo accounts are pre-created and **shared**, issued on request; no self-registration, no admin role, no MFA | `docker-compose.prod.yml`, `deploy/Caddyfile`, `docs/DEPLOYMENT-PROD.md` § 3c, `frontend/src/auth.ts` |
| Browser storage | `sessionStorage`: `arenibus.oidc.tokens`, `arenibus.oidc.verifier`, `arenibus.oidc.state`. `localStorage`: `arenibus.theme`, `arenibus.setupguide`, `arenibus.guide`/`hdguide`/`pdguide.<patientId>`, `arenibus.summary.dokumentacia`, `arenibus.patients.programme` — UI preferences only, no patient data | `frontend/src/auth.ts`, `ui/theme.ts`, `components/*Guide.tsx`, `pages/PatientsPage.tsx`, `pages/patient/SummaryTab.tsx` |
| Cookies | The app sets none — verified: no `Set-Cookie` on `/`. Keycloak sets its own technical session cookies under `/auth` during login | live `curl -I` |
| Audit log | Append-only. Stores `UserId`, `OccurredAtUtc`, `Action`, `EntityType`, `EntityId`, optional JSON `Details`. Failed attempts (`access.denied`, `access.missing_record`) store method, status and request path; unauthenticated 401s are not recorded. **No IP addresses and no request bodies** | `src/Arenibus.Common.Persistence/AuditEntry.cs`, `src/Arenibus.Modules.Audit/Endpoints/AccessAttemptAuditMiddleware.cs` |
| IP addresses | Only transient, in memory, as the rate-limiter partition key (`RateLimiting:PermitPerMinute`, default 300/min). Caddy declares **no** `log` directive → no HTTP access log | `src/Arenibus.Api/Program.cs`, `deploy/Caddyfile` |
| Service logs | Docker `json-file` driver, `max-size: 10m` × `max-file: 5` per service | `docker-compose.prod.yml` `x-logging` |
| Hosting | `demo.arenibus.com` → `46.225.227.88` → RDAP RIPE `CLOUD-NBG1`, **Hetzner Online GmbH**, Industriestrasse 25, 91710 Gunzenhausen DE; Nuremberg DC (EU) | RDAP `rdap.db.ripe.net` |
| Recipients | Hetzner only. API, PostgreSQL and Keycloak publish no host ports — only Caddy's 80/443. `OTEL_EXPORTER_OTLP_ENDPOINT` defaults empty, `NCZI_ENABLED` defaults false (no NCZI certificate issued yet), ÚDZS credentials unset. Formspree is marketing-site-only | `docker-compose.prod.yml` |
| Transfers | None outside the EU/EEA | hosting + recipients above |
| Nightly reset | 03:00 cron runs `deploy/demo-reset.sh`: `pg_restore --clean --if-exists` of the `arenibus` database from a golden snapshot, then shifts appointments and dialysis procedures to current dates. **The Keycloak database is NOT restored — accounts survive.** The day's audit rows are overwritten along with everything else | `deploy/demo-reset.sh`, `docs/DEPLOYMENT-PROD.md` § 3c |
| Backups | `age`-encrypted before upload, EU object storage; a single target is permitted for demo | `deploy/backup.sh`, `docs/DEPLOYMENT-PROD.md` § 4 |

#### Verification performed
- `npm run lint`: clean.
- `npm run build`: static export succeeded.
- `npm run check:contrast`: all token pairs pass WCAG 2.1 AA in both themes; dark-palette parity holds.
- `npm run check:links`: all pass, including the two new `#demo` fragments on `/privacy/`.
- Generated `out/privacy/index.html`: exactly one `<h1>`, no skipped heading levels (`h2` → `h3`),
  `id="demo"` present, meta description 151 characters.

#### Self-improvement notes
- The demo's privacy and terms links are compiled into its frontend build
  (`frontend/src/App.tsx` → `https://arenibus.polascin.net/privacy/` and `/terms/`). Renaming or
  moving either marketing route silently breaks the demo's legal links until the demo is rebuilt.
- Legal copy must be traced to deployed configuration, not to intent. The nightly reset is a
  **snapshot restore**, so calling it a deletion of accounts and logs would have been wrong in both
  directions: Keycloak accounts survive it, while the day's audit rows do not.
- Before starting an audit run, `git fetch` and confirm the local checkout is not behind
  `origin/main`. This run was begun 27 commits behind and the edits had to be redone against the
  current `app/privacy/page.tsx` (which had since moved its metadata into a `PRIVACY_DESCRIPTION`
  constant) — the same staleness that CI's sync check guards against.

The lockfile already has `braces@^3.0.3`; no 3.0.4 is on npm. `npm audit fix --force` proposes `eslint-config-next@14.2.35`, which is incompatible with Next.js 16.3.8. - **Recommendation:** Leave the pin. Re-evaluate when `braces@3.0.4+` or a Next 16.3.x eslint plugin that bumps micromatch/braces publishes. Do not use `npm audit fix --force`.
- **Fixed:** Documented under Known Security Exceptions. No dependency downgrade.

- **Severity:** Low
- **Category:** Accessibility

The mobile-menu toggle used a combined „Otvoriť alebo zavrieť menu“ label instead of a state-specific name.
- **Recommendation:** Keep the short visible text (2.5.3 Label in Name) and set `aria-label` to `Zobraziť viac: {title}` / `Zobraziť menej: {title}`. Name the menu toggle `Otvoriť menu` or `Zavrieť menu` from `aria-expanded`.
- **Fixed:** Feature expand buttons now include the module title in `aria-label`. The mobile toggle uses a state-specific Slovak `aria-label`.

#### Summary
- Critical: 0
- High: 0
- Medium: 1
- Low: 1
- Info: 1

#### Findings

##### 1. Clarity consent dialog lacked modal focus management
- **Severity:** Medium
- **Category:** Accessibility
- **File(s):** `public/clarity-consent.js`, `public/clarity-consent.css`, `app/layout.tsx`, `scripts/check-contrast.mjs`
- **Description:** The banner used `role="dialog"` and `aria-modal="true"` but did not move focus into the dialog on first paint, did not trap Tab, and did not restore focus when dismissed. Accept and Decline looked identical. Grok confirmed the three focus rules for a WCAG-compliant consent modal.
- **Recommendation:** Focus the first control on open, trap Tab inside the dialog, restore the previously focused control on close, style Accept as the primary action, and contrast-check the new pairs.
- **Fixed:** Dialog focuses the container on open (announces the title), traps Tab/Shift+Tab, marks sibling body children `inert` while open, restores focus to Analytics settings (or the opener), styles `.clarity-consent-accept`, adds light/dark accept contrast pairs, cache-busts to `?v=20261105b`. Grok follow-up also flagged missing `inert` and restore-to-settings after auto-open.

##### 2. Contact form did not expose busy state to assistive tech
- **Severity:** Low
- **Category:** Accessibility
- **File(s):** `app/contact-form.tsx`
- **Description:** Submit set `disabled` while posting to Formspree, but the form itself had no `aria-busy`, so screen readers did not get a clear busy state beyond the button label change.
- **Recommendation:** Set `aria-busy={formStatus === "submitting"}` on the form.
- **Fixed:** Form now sets `aria-busy` while submitting.

##### 3. Demo hash, version and clinician nav are unchanged
- **Severity:** Info
- **Category:** Content
- **File(s):** live `https://demo.arenibus.com/`, `lib/site.ts`, `lib/features.ts`
- **Description:** Same bundle hash and `0.17.264` as Run 63/64. No leftover catalog waits for a tile. Sitemap `<lastmod>` already `2026-11-05`.
- **Recommendation:** Keep `DEMO_VERSION` at `v0.17.264`. Spend the run on a11y.
- **Fixed:** Version left at `v0.17.264`.

#### Fixed in this run
- Clarity consent modal: focus-in, Tab trap, focus restore, primary Accept styling, contrast pairs, cache-bust `20261105b`.
- Contact form exposes `aria-busy` while submitting.
- Local install refreshed to `next@16.4.5` via `npm ci` after pull left 16.4.3 in `node_modules`.

#### Verification performed
- Demo HTML `Last-Modified: Tue, 03 Nov 2026 13:12:04 GMT`; `/assets/index-BpwuMTUU.js` 289 712 bytes; unique version `0.17.264`. Clinician nav unchanged. Lazy chunks parsed; no new leftover catalog for tiles.
- Grok CLI (headless `-p`): confirmed consent-dialog focus-in, Tab trap and focus restore as the three WCAG points to fix.
- `npm ci` then `next@16.4.5` installed.
- `npm run lint`: clean.
- `npm run check:contrast`: all token pairs plus expand-hover pairs plus eight Clarity pairs (including accept light/dark) pass WCAG 2.1 AA; dark palettes stay in sync.
- `npx next build --webpack`: Next.js 16.4.5 webpack static export.
- `npm run check:links`: all internal, external, fragments, mailto, PHP redirects and sitemap URLs pass.
- `npm audit --omit=dev`: 0.
- Live production before deploy: Chrome-UA `/` HTTP 200, `v0.17.264`, expand labels present (Clarity a11y from this run lands after deploy).

#### Self-improvement notes
- After `git pull` that bumps Next, always `npm ci` before local webpack build — Windows can keep the previous `node_modules/next` until then (this run still printed 16.4.3 until `npm ci`).
- `aria-modal="true"` without focus-in / Tab trap / focus restore is an incomplete dialog pattern; treat it as Medium even when the visual banner looks finished.
- Grok CLI with `--cwd` on this repo or with very long pasted file bodies can hang; prefer a short headless `-p` prompt, then implement locally.
- Value/label substring matching against marketing copy is noisy (brackets, UI chrome). Prefer comparing official catalog labels already tracked in Known Project-Specific Checks before editing tiles.
- `next@16.4.5` stays the November security train. Canaries remain at `16.5.0-canary.41`.
- Space production smoke GETs to avoid OpenResty 429.
- Grok (delayed headless pass after Run 65): unlayered custom `.sr-only` in `globals.css` beats Tailwind `focus:not-sr-only` and keeps skip links clipped — remove the custom block. Also flagged JSON-LD free-offer fields, duplicated subpage headers, and redundant MVP glossary lines under features (candidates for Run 66+).

- **Severity:** Low

- **Severity:** Low

- **File(s):** `app/contact-form.tsx`
- **Description:** Run 65 set `aria-busy` and disabled only the submit button. Name / e-mail / message stayed editable during the Formspree POST, so a user could change values mid-submit.
- **Recommendation:** Wrap the visible fields in a `fieldset` with `disabled` while `formStatus === "submitting"` and keep a visible (sr-only) legend.
- **Fixed:** Contact inputs sit in a submitting-disabled `fieldset` with legend „Kontaktné údaje“. Submit stays outside so its own disabled + label change remain the primary busy control.

### Run 90 — 2026-11-06 (Skip-link stacking, 404 IČO, opaque legal callouts)

#### Scope & Intent
- Daily cron `.doaudit.md` (2026-11-06 01:03 UTC). Demo still `v0.17.264` (`index-BpwuMTUU.js`, Last-Modified 2026-11-03 13:12:04 GMT, 289 712 bytes, `Yp="0.17.264"`). Parsed leftover catalogs plus lazy `PatientsPage-DbyophUN.js` (deactivation list). No new clinician nav tiles; leftover strings are UI chrome or already on the cards. `next@16.4.5` remains latest stable; canaries at `16.5.0-canary.42`. Production `npm audit --omit=dev` is 0; full audit still 8 moderate `minimatch` findings (do not force-fix). Rebased onto Run 89 `NotFoundShell` before committing.

#### Findings
##### 1. Skip link sat under the sticky header with a brand-on-brand outline
- **Severity:** Medium
- **Category:** a11y
- **File(s):** `app/skip-link.tsx`
- **Description:** Focused skip control used `absolute` + `z-50`, the same stacking context as the sticky `h-16` header that follows it in the DOM. `focus:not-sr-only` also sets `position: static`, so it raced `focus:absolute`. Global `:focus-visible` outline is `--brand` on a `--brand` chip, so the ring disappeared.
- **Fixed:** `focus:!fixed focus:z-[100]` (above the header, unambiguous over `not-sr-only`) and a `brand-text` outline matching primary buttons.

##### 2. Standalone 404 document omitted `formatDetection.telephone`
- **Severity:** Low
- **Category:** SEO / UX
- **File(s):** `app/global-not-found.tsx`
- **Description:** In-layout 404 inherits root metadata (`formatDetection.telephone: false`). `out/404.html` is a separate document (`global-not-found` bypasses the layout) and showed footer IČO `57646856` without that hint, so mobile browsers could auto-link it as a phone number. Run 89 shared the chrome; the metadata still has to be set on this file.
- **Fixed:** Same `formatDetection` on `GlobalNotFound` metadata.

##### 3. Legal scope callouts used a translucent surface
- **Severity:** Low
- **Category:** a11y / contrast
- **File(s):** `app/privacy/page.tsx`, `app/terms/page.tsx`
- **Description:** Scope boxes used `bg-surface-2/80` over the `section-soft` teal wash. Solid-token contrast checks cannot see the composite; the same class of defect as the old demo-band overlays.
- **Fixed:** Opaque `bg-surface-2`.

##### 4. Desktop section links missed the shared focus ring
- **Severity:** Low
- **Category:** a11y / DX
- **File(s):** `app/site-header.tsx`
- **Description:** After the prose/footer ring sweep, homepage desktop `#features` / `#demo` / `#contact` links still relied only on `nav-link` underline + the global outline.
- **Fixed:** `nav-link` plus `mutedLinkClass` (underline kept).

#### Verification
- lint / contrast / webpack build / links; spaced production smoke (homepage + legal + robots/sitemap). Demo version left at `v0.17.264`.

#### Self-improvement
- Skip controls that share a z-index with a later sticky header need `fixed` + a higher z-index, and must not rely on `not-sr-only` for positioning.
- Brand-colored skip chips need a `brand-text` outline, same as `.btn-primary:focus-visible`.
- `app/global-not-found.tsx` is a full document: copy root metadata hints that affect the shared footer (telephone format-detection) whenever the in-layout 404 already inherits them. Sharing `NotFoundShell` does not copy metadata.
- After a shared-class sweep, re-grep homepage desktop nav — section links are not legal prose and were easy to miss.
- When demo HTML Last-Modified and `/assets/index-*.js` hash are unchanged, still fetch `PatientsPage-*.js` (lazy, not inlined) before declaring no leftover deactivation catalog.

- **Severity:** Medium
- **Category:** a11y

- **Severity:** Low
- **Category:** DX
- **File(s):** `app/page.tsx`
- **Description:** Same leftover pattern already cleaned from the contact error link in Run 91.

- **Severity:** Medium
- **Category:** a11y

- **Description:** „Preskočiť na hlavný obsah“ only scrolled to `<main id="main-content">`. Without `tabIndex={-1}`, many browsers leave keyboard focus on the skip control (or the document), so the next Tab still walks the sticky header instead of the page body (WCAG 2.4.1 technique G1).

#### Self-improvement

Activating those anchors scrolled the section under the sticky nav but left keyboard focus on the link, so the next Tab returned to the header instead of continuing in the section (same WCAG 2.4.1 / G1 class as Run 95).
- **Fixed:** `tabIndex={-1}` on those sections; outline suppression extended to `#features` / `#demo` / `#contact`.

##### 2. Demo access card still used a translucent border token
- **Severity:** Low
- **Category:** a11y / DX
- **File(s):** `app/page.tsx`
- **Description:** `border-border/60` on the opaque demo credentials card was leftover from the old frosted overlays.
- **Fixed:** Solid `border-border`.

#### Verification
- lint / contrast / webpack build / links; production smoke after deploy. Demo version left at `v0.17.264`.

#### Self-improvement
- Any in-page `href="#…"` used for keyboard navigation needs the same `tabIndex={-1}` treatment as the skip target, not only `scroll-padding-top`.
- After an opaque-surface contrast pass, re-grep `/[0-9]+` border/background utilities on those cards.

## 📝 Known Project-Specific Checks (fill during runs)

- Hero heading must remain `Nefrologický a dialyzačný informačný systém`, with the MVP version badge and the advanced-development notice nearby.
- The demo links „Ochrana osobných údajov“ and „Podmienky používania“ from its login screen and landing footer to `https://arenibus.polascin.net/privacy/` and `/terms/` (hardcoded in `frontend/src/App.tsx` of `arenibus-nis`). It does not link `/security/`. `/privacy/` covers the demo in article 7 (effective 2026-10-02; do not reopen that article) and sends security reports to `/security/`. `/security/` therefore receives reports for both arenibus.polascin.net and demo.arenibus.com at arenibus@polascin.net. `/terms/` states that articles 2, 3, 4, 5 and 6 apply to the demo, and article 7 applies to the whole text including those demo articles; article 1 is the marketing site. Keep the ban on real patient data and the nightly 03:00 Europe/Bratislava snapshot restore (the day's entries including audit rows are overwritten; login accounts and service logs are not deleted). Never reintroduce a blanket „na demo sa tieto zásady nevzťahujú“ or a security scope limited to the marketing site, and never rename or move these routes without a demo release.
- Article 7 of `/privacy/` must stay true to the demo's deployed configuration (`D:/Dev/arenibus`: `docker-compose.prod.yml`, `deploy/Caddyfile`, `deploy/demo-reset.sh`, `docs/DEPLOYMENT-PROD.md`, `frontend/src/auth.ts`). Re-check it whenever hosting, the OIDC flow, browser-storage keys, logging, or the nightly reset change. As of 2026-10-02: Hetzner Nuremberg, Keycloak OIDC with shared pre-created accounts, no app cookies, audit without IP addresses, IP only in memory for rate limiting, no web access log, no transfers outside the EU/EEA.
- The nightly 03:00 demo reset is a `pg_restore` of the `arenibus` database from a golden snapshot. It overwrites the day's data **including audit rows**, and it does **not** touch the Keycloak account database or the service logs. Never describe it as deleting accounts or logs.
- `DEMO_VERSION` in `lib/site.ts` (imported by `app/page.tsx` and JSON-LD in `app/layout.tsx`) must match the version shown on https://demo.arenibus.com/ (footer `Arenibus vX.Y.Z`). Do not leave the marketing badges or `softwareVersion` on a previous patch. As of Run 103 the live pin is still **v0.17.288** (`index-jIMNcg3p.js`, Last-Modified 2026-11-10 07:53:56 GMT).
