- As of Run 63 the v0.17.264 catalogs plus adjacent maps are folded into existing tiles (not new tiles): patient deactivation with optional reason and reactivation (kartotéka deaktivovaných nezobrazuje; rodné číslo ostáva jedinečné); `úmrtie stornované` (dohoda ostáva zaniknutá); OFF-LABEL prescribing (MZ SR č. Z074902-2026; „Rp.“ under dosage; does not itself create a VZP entitlement — zák. 363/2011 request); US / MRI are not ionizing radiation; confidentiality-release `označenie listiny` and revocation with osvedčený podpis (§ 18 ods. 4); referral batch fields (kód PZS pole 23 / kód lekára pole 24, deň vystavenia, predbežné stanovenie choroby, rozsah a cieľ, identifikátor záznamu v EZK); dedicated paid-care notification § 79 ods. 1 písm. ap (elektronicky, lehota, evidencia; bez vyplnenej poisťovne); invoice columns vystavená / splatnosť / fakturované. Earlier Run 62 leftovers stay on the cards. No leftover catalog currently waits for a tile.
- As of Run 62 the leftover v0.17.256 catalogs plus adjacent maps stay folded: EU `druh nároku nezapísaný v evidencii`; professional-vaccination checkbox `hradí poisťovňa (políčko ostáva)` / `neuhrádza poisťovňa (políčko prečiarknuté)`; death source `úmrtný list (matrika)`; náprava applicant `osoba sama (§ 17 ods. 1)` / `blízka osoba (§ 17 ods. 2)` and official results (`žiadosti sa vyhovelo` / `žiadosti sa nevyhovelo` / `žiadateľ vzal žiadosť späť`); confidentiality-release addressee (`osoba, ktorej sa skutočnosti týkajú` / `orgán, ktorý vydal povolenie`); biomed participant (`účastník výskumu` / `zákonný zástupca`); retention disposal including vyradenie v registratúrnom konaní (zák. č. 395/2002 Z. z.), odovzdanie inému poskytovateľovi (§ 23 ods. 3) and úschova krajom (§ 23 ods. 4 a 5); drug origin `pôvod nezapísaný`; emergency confirmation `potvrdené ako neodkladná starostlivosť` plus reason bod 1 (bez prihlášky) / bod 2 (poisťovňa bez zmluvy). Earlier Run 61 leftovers stay on the cards.
- As of Run 60 the leftover v0.17.255 catalogs plus adjacent maps stay folded: DZS exemption a–h spelled out (a) chronický dialyzačný / odberový / transplantačný program, b) onkologická alebo kardiochirurgická liečba, c) ŤZP — odkázanosť na individuálnu prepravu, d) preprava medzi ústavnými zariadeniami, e) najmenej zlatá Janského plaketa, f) dieťa podľa rozhodnutia súdu, g) sociálna služba v pobytovom zariadení, h) sociálna služba krízovej intervencie) plus smer (obojsmerne na ošetrenie a späť / do zdravotníckeho zariadenia / zo zdravotníckeho zariadenia / medzi zdravotníckymi zariadeniami); visit refund codes 01 úraz zavinený cudzou osobou / 02 pracovný úraz / 03 choroba z povolania / 04 alkohol / omamné látky / 06 porušenie liečebného režimu (05 / 07 / 08 recorded-only); ÚDZS lookup outcomes (poistenec nájdený / nenájdený / volanie zlyhalo); written-form § 20 ods. 2 four reasons; related-service official titles; lab origin official titles plus identifikátor záznamu / poznámka k odberu; BMI I. (1. stupňa) / II. (2. stupňa) / III. (morbídna); infectious possible-case (podozrenie); iné zneužívanie; poučená osoba zákonný zástupca alebo iná oprávnená osoba; HD oprava dovolená do verifikácie, zrušené záznamy ošetrenia (kedy, kto a prečo) and plán ošetrení z režimu; ops notices (zastupovanie počas dočasnej neprítomnosti / dočasné pozastavenie / skončenie poskytovania ZS). Earlier Run 59 leftovers stay on the cards. No leftover catalog currently waits for a tile.
- Features section heading must read: `Funkcie nefrologického a dialyzačného informačného systému Arenibus v aktuálnej verzii` with `(MVP = Minimum Viable Product)` and Slovak translation `(Minimálny životaschopný produkt)` on separate lines.
- Brand color must remain teal/petrol: `#0f766e` (light), `#2dd4bf` (dark mode brand).
- Dark mode toggle must persist preference in `localStorage` and respect system preference on first load. Implemented via `useSyncExternalStore` in `app/theme-toggle.tsx` to avoid `setState` in effect lint errors. Subscribe to `matchMedia` as well when no stored preference exists. Mount the same control on every sticky nav via `SiteHeader` (`variant="home"` on `/`, `variant="subpage"` on `/privacy/`, `/terms/`, `/security/`, and `app/not-found.tsx`). Do not re-copy the sticky header markup onto legal pages.
- `SiteHeader` `variant="subpage"` is logo + theme toggle + „Späť na úvod“ only. Never put homepage `#features` / `#demo` / `#contact` anchors or the mobile menu on subpages. Skip links stay declared inline on each page (not inside `SiteHeader`).
- Demo CTA button text must be clearly visible in both light and dark modes. Never use `text-white` / `hover:text-white` on the demo brand band; use `text-brand-text` (dark `--brand` `#2dd4bf` fails AA against white).
- Small text on the demo band must sit on an opaque `surface` / `surface-2` card, including the `text-xs` version pill (`bg-surface text-brand-strong`). Do not reintroduce `bg-surface/10` (or `/20`) overlays with `text-brand-text/90` or nested `opacity-75` — composited contrast fails WCAG AA in light mode and the solid-token checker will not catch it.
- Homepage `app/page.tsx` is a Server Component. Mobile nav lives in `app/site-header.tsx` and the Formspree form in `app/contact-form.tsx`. Do not put `"use client"` back on the homepage.
- JSON-LD `SoftwareApplication` must include `featureList` for the ten clinician modules (not admin-only screens), `softwareVersion`, `inLanguage: "sk"`, square `logo` (`/icon-512.png`), and optional `image` for the 1200×630 OG card. Do not claim free access via `offers` / `isAccessibleForFree` while demo credentials are on request; do not put the demo URL in `installUrl` or `sameAs`. Emit `SoftwareApplication` and `WebSite` only on the homepage (`app/site-json-ld.tsx` via `app/page.tsx`); legal pages and 404 must not inherit those nodes. Legal pages must also emit `BreadcrumbList`. Homepage-only `alternates.canonical: "/"` and `openGraph.url` belong on `app/page.tsx`, not the root layout; `app/not-found.tsx` must set title/description and must not claim a home canonical.
- Do not put `"Inter Variable"` or another unloaded webfont first in `body` `font-family`.
- Feature cards must use `h3` (not `h4`) to maintain correct heading hierarchy under the section `h2`.
- Contact form must have a **visible** `<label>` for every input (not only `sr-only`). Name and e-mail fields must also set `autoComplete="name"` and `autoComplete="email"` (WCAG 1.3.5 / 3.3.2). Keep a `mailto:arenibus@polascin.net` fallback (and `<noscript>`) because parent CSP may block Formspree. Do not set a Formspree `action` attribute. On success or error, move focus into the visible status/`alert` node (`tabIndex={-1}`); do not rely on polite `aria-live` alone after submit. Footer links with `aria-current="page"` must also use a visible weight/colour cue (`font-semibold text-foreground`).
- Root metadata must set `formatDetection.telephone: false` so the footer IČO is not auto-linked as a phone number.
- Every route with a sticky nav (`/`, `/privacy/`, `/terms/`, `/security/`, and 404) must offer a skip link „Preskočiť na hlavný obsah“ targeting `#main-content` on that same page (shared `app/skip-link.tsx` is allowed; `check:links` resolves fragments via importing `page.tsx`). Put that `id` on `<main tabIndex={-1}>` (not a hero `<section>`), wrap the sticky nav in `<header>`, and keep a `<nav aria-label="Hlavná navigácia">`. Suppress the global `:focus-visible` outline on `#main-content` so skip focus does not ring the whole page. Shared footer chrome (`app/site-footer.tsx`) is allowed; footer legal links must live in `<nav aria-label="Pätičkové odkazy">` and include Úvod. Each feature card `<article>` must expose `aria-labelledby` pointing at its `h3`. `html` must set `scroll-padding-top` (currently 5rem) so `#features`, `#demo`, `#contact`, and `#main-content` clear the sticky `h-16` nav. The skip control must paint above the sticky header (`focus:!fixed focus:z-[100]`, not `z-50` / `absolute` racing `not-sr-only`) and use a `brand-text` outline on the brand chip. Homepage desktop section links keep `nav-link` plus `mutedLinkClass`. Sticky nav and legal scope callouts stay opaque `bg-surface` / `bg-surface-2` (no `/90` or `/80` wash over page atmospheres). Contact fields use `fieldFocusClass` (`outline-none` + brand ring). Theme bootstrap lives in `lib/theme-bootstrap.ts` and is imported by both `app/layout.tsx` and `app/global-not-found.tsx`. `app/global-not-found.tsx` must set `formatDetection.telephone: false` itself — it does not inherit root layout metadata even after Run 89 shared `NotFoundShell`. Homepage JSON-LD OG card dimensions come from `SITE_OG_IMAGE`.
- Dark-mode toggle must keep `aria-pressed` (boolean) and a state-specific Slovak `aria-label` (`Zapnúť tmavý režim` when light / `Zapnúť svetlý režim` when dark) on every route that has a sticky nav.
- Every user-facing `<a target="_blank">` on the marketing site (homepage demo CTAs and legal-page external links including demo.arenibus.com, Microsoft privacy, and dataprotection.gov.sk) must include a screen-reader-only hint ` (otvorí sa v novom okne)`. `npm run check:links` must fail if that hint is missing from an external `_blank` anchor body.
- Dependabot alert on `minimatch` (GHSA-g3hv-75wg-m249 / CVE-2026-118764, medium): do **not** pin `minimatch@11` via overrides while eslint 9 still requires the 3.x/9.x API surface. Track only; do not run `npm audit fix --force`.
- Mobile menu must support `Escape` key to close (and restore focus to the toggle). The `#mobile-menu` node must stay inside `<nav aria-label="Hlavná navigácia">` whenever the toggle declares `aria-controls="mobile-menu"` (toggle with `hidden` / CSS, do not conditional-mount). Opening the menu moves focus to the first item and that item must show a visible focus ring even after programmatic `.focus()` (`programmaticFocusRingClass`, not only `:focus-visible`). While open, Tab must cycle between the toggle and the menu links (focus trap); mark `#main-content`, `footer`, the brand wordmark link, and the theme-toggle wrapper `inert` until close. Crossing `min-width: 768px` must set `mobileMenuOpen` false so `inert`/trap do not linger under desktop chrome. Activating a mobile `#features` / `#demo` / `#contact` link must close the panel then move focus onto that section (`focusSectionAfterMenuClose`), not leave focus on the now-hidden menu anchor. Desktop section links, the hero „Kontaktujte nás“ CTA, the shared `SkipLink` (`#main-content`), and privacy in-page „článok 7“ / „článku 7“ (`#demo`) use shared `HashLink` / `focusHashTarget` so keyboard focus leaves the activator (same WCAG 2.4.1 / G1 class). Breadcrumb current crumb (`aria-current="page"`) keeps a visible weight/colour cue (`font-semibold text-foreground`), same rule as footer chrome. `.btn-primary` / `.btn-secondary` keep a dual-tone focus ring (surface halo + brand), not a lone `brand-text` outline that disappears on `bg-surface` cards. Brand wordmark link and menu toggle keep `focus-visible` rings when the menu is closed. Nav logo image next to the visible „Arenibus“ wordmark must use decorative `alt=""`. Absolute marketing origin is `SITE_ORIGIN` in `lib/site.ts` (metadataBase, JSON-LD, breadcrumbs, homepage `openGraph.url`). Shared card art is `SITE_OG_IMAGE` (root + legal openGraph/twitter).
- All decorative SVG icons must use `aria-hidden="true"`.
- SEO files must exist in `public/`: `robots.txt`, `sitemap.xml`, `manifest.json`. The user-facing security contact is `/security/` (`app/security/page.tsx`), linked from the privacy page. Do not link `/.well-known/security.txt` or `/security.txt` on this host — OpenResty 404s dot-directories and WAF 403s `security.txt`. Unknown paths use branded `out/404.html` via `public/.htaccess` `ErrorDocument 404 /404.html` plus `experimental.globalNotFound` / `app/global-not-found.tsx` (clean title, no homepage canonical/OG/JSON-LD).
- Homepage JSON-LD `SoftwareApplication` must include `softwareVersion` (plain `X.Y.Z` from `DEMO_VERSION_PLAIN`), `inLanguage: "sk"`, and an absolute square `logo` URL (`icon-512.png`, not `og-image.png`). Do not emit that node (or `WebSite`) from the root layout onto `/privacy/`, `/terms/`, `/security/`, or 404.
- When a parent directory contains another lockfile, `next.config.ts` must set `turbopack.root` to the project root so builds do not infer the wrong workspace.
- With `output: 'export'`, `npm start` must serve `out/` through a static file server rather than invoking `next start`.
- Contact-form success and error feedback must use an ARIA live region. Errors use `role="alert"` and `aria-live="assertive"`; success and the submitting state use `role="status"` / `polite` (submitting announces „Odosielam správu.“). Breadcrumb separator `/` must not be its own `<li>`; keep it `aria-hidden` inside the home crumb item.
- Meta description must stay ≤ 160 characters on every route; verify generated `out/index.html`, `out/privacy/index.html`, and `out/terms/index.html` (a homepage-only check missed a 165-character terms description).
- All `aria-label` attributes on interactive controls must use Slovak copy (e.g., dark-mode toggle, mobile menu).
- `public/sitemap.xml` `<lastmod>` should be refreshed on each release/deploy **for every `<loc>`**, not only the homepage.
- Demo credentials must never appear in plain text on the marketing site or anywhere in this public repository. The site offers demo access on request via arenibus@nephroctor.com; the actual credentials live only in the private `arenibus-nis` repo and Keycloak (OIDC).
- Marketing page must display a clear notice that the information system is still in advanced development (e.g., a hero badge and demo-section wording).
- Image assets must be audited by **weight and real dimensions**, not just existence: no single asset should exceed ~200 KB, and the declared `og:image` width/height must match the actual file.
- The purpose-built image set must stay in place: `logo-768.webp` (hero), `logo-96.webp` (nav), `og-image.png` (1200x630 social card), `apple-touch-icon.png` (180x180), `icon-192.png` / `icon-512.png` (manifest). Regenerate from the 2048x2048 master kept in git history (`git show c0af678:public/ArenibusLogo.png`) if the branding changes. Hero `logo-768.webp` next to the brand H1 must use decorative `alt=""` (same rule as the nav wordmark). Homepage `#features`, `#demo`, and `#contact` sections must set `aria-labelledby` to their `h2` ids.
- `npm run check:contrast` must pass. It parses `app/globals.css` directly and also asserts the two dark blocks (`[data-theme="dark"]` and `prefers-color-scheme: dark`) stay in sync. Add a row to its `PAIRS` table whenever a new foreground/background combination appears in the markup.
- `npm run check:links` must pass after `npm run build`. It parses `<a>` / `<Link>` / `<HashLink>` `href`, `fetch()` URLs (string literals or file-level `const` identifiers), and `<form action>` from `app/**/*.tsx` (not Open Graph, Twitter Card, canonical, or JSON-LD URLs), PHP `Location` headers, and sitemap `<loc>` entries. Internal paths must exist in `out/` with a trailing slash; fragments must match an `id` on the declaring file **or** on a `page.tsx` that imports that file (homepage header islands); external HTTP(S) URLs must return 2xx/3xx (Formspree GET may return 405); `mailto:` addresses must be `arenibus@polascin.net` or `arenibus@nephroctor.com`; external `<a>` tags must use `target="_blank"`, `rel="noopener noreferrer"`, and the screen-reader hint `otvorí sa v novom okne` inside the anchor body. Broken links found in an audit must be fixed in the same run. Do not maintain a manual URL allowlist — new links are picked up from source. Network-layer failures (timeout / `fetch failed`) and gateway outages (HTTP 502/503/504) on `dataprotection.gov.sk` after retries are a documented CI exception and must log `WARN`, not fail the job; HTTP 404/410 from that host remains a failure.
- Layout regressions must be confirmed by measuring `documentElement.scrollWidth` against `clientWidth` inside the page at ~360 px, not by eyeballing a headless screenshot.
- Hero media must stay fluid (`w-full max-w-96 aspect-square`); never reintroduce a fixed `w-96`, which exceeds a 360 px viewport minus the section padding. The hero `<section>` must set `aria-labelledby="hero-heading"` with that id on the brand H1 (same landmark pattern as features/demo/contact).
- Every inline legal/contact `target="_blank"` and in-prose mailto/fragment link should use `proseLinkClass` (or `mutedLinkClass` on softer homepage contact text). After a shared-class sweep, re-grep bare external anchors — Run 87 caught the Microsoft privacy statement left without the ring class.
- `deploy.sh` and `.github/workflows/deploy.yml` must stay in agreement: both need a guarded `cd`, backups written **outside** the wiped directory, and `find`-based cleanup and permission steps. The live smoke test must use a full Chrome User-Agent and check `/`, `/privacy/`, `/terms/`, `/security/`, `/robots.txt`, and `/sitemap.xml` (parent WAF 466s short UAs on several of those paths).
- Deployment backups must land in `$HOME/arenibus-backups/` with retention, never inside the deploy directory.
- `app/favicon.ico` (App Router convention) shadows `public/favicon.ico`; keep only the `app/` copy.
- A blocking `themeInit` script in `<head>` must apply `data-theme` before first paint; the CSS `prefers-color-scheme` block remains the no-JS fallback.
- Footer copyright must use the same year range as the demo (`Arenibus © 2024–2026 Ľubomír Polaščín` as of 2026-09-12).
- When the demo JS minifies away `im="X.Y.Z"` / `ip="X.Y.Z"`, treat a unique `X.Y.Z` version string in `/assets/index-*.js` (cross-checked against the landing HTML `Last-Modified` and bundle filename) as the source of truth. The minified binding name changes between deploys (`im`, `ip`, `fp`, `Sp`, `Up`, `jp`, `Hp`, `Yp`, …). When the hash changes, also fetch `birthNumberPlausibility-*.js` and `types-*.js` — RČ plausibility (§ 21 ods. 6) and visit payer catalogs live there.
- Framework pin as of Run 103 is `next@16.4.6` + `eslint-config-next@16.4.6` + `react@19.3.1`. Adopt newer 16.4.x patches; canaries are at `16.5.0-canary.48`. Dev tooling: `tailwindcss@4.3.5`, `@tailwindcss/postcss@4.3.5`, `@types/react@19.3.0`, `@types/react-dom@19.3.0`, `serve@14.2.7`.
- Hero `.motion-fade-up*` must set `animation: none` under `prefers-reduced-motion`. A global `animation-duration: 0.01ms` still flashes the `from` keyframe because those classes use `animation-fill-mode: both`.
- Local builds on the Windows workstation must use `npx next build --webpack` (WDAC blocks the native SWC binary); leave `npm run build` on Turbopack for CI.
- Keep `package.json` `overrides` for `source-map-js@1.2.2`, `compression@1.8.2`, and `sharp@0.35.5` until Next / Tailwind / `serve` pull those patches themselves. Do not drop them just because the static export never ships those parsers or the `next/image` optimizer to the browser.

## ⚠️ Known Security Exceptions

- **None open in production as of 2026-11-12 (Run 103).** `npm audit --omit=dev` reports 0 vulnerabilities on `next@16.4.6` after `overrides` pin `source-map-js@1.2.2` (GHSA-68fv-2mgg-jv7q / CVE-2026-93749; the 1.2.1 parser is build-time PostCSS only), `compression@1.8.2` (GHSA-vc2v-76pw-4v95, local `serve` only), `sharp@0.35.5` (GHSA-wq5f-xc86-pv6w / CVE-2026-96889; optional `next/image` optimizer, unused because `images.unoptimized: true`), `zod@4.6.7` (GHSA-mrhx-c7xc-m3w5; lint-toolchain via `eslint-plugin-react-hooks`), and `fast-uri@3.1.9` (GHSA-w74x-v6f9-j3xg and related; local `serve` → `ajv` only). Full `npm audit` still reports 8 moderate findings, all GHSA-g3hv-75wg-m249 (`minimatch` < 11.0.1 / CVE-2026-118764) in eslint / typescript-eslint. The advisory text describes `firstPhasePreProcess` in the 10.x line; pinning every `minimatch` to 11.x would break eslint 9's 3.x API, and `npm audit fix --force` wants eslint 4.0.0 or serve 6.5.8 — not applied. `braces@3.0.3` remains in the lint tree via `fast-glob` → `micromatch` but is no longer reported. This static export does not use Cache Components, Server Actions, or the image optimizer. Stay on `16.4.6` unless a newer 16.4.x patch ships.
- `https://dataprotection.gov.sk/` is the official ÚOOÚ URL and remains on the privacy page. GitHub Actions IPs are occasionally dropped at the network layer (`fetch failed` / timeout) and the host itself sometimes answers 502/503/504. `scripts/check-links.mjs` retries then logs `WARN` for that host only; HTTP 404/410 still fail the job. Observed on run `34549407421` (2026-09-11, network) and Run 47 (2026-10-16, HTTP 503).
- Next.js **feature** releases are adopted only after a 7-day vetting window. **Critical security patches skip that window**. `next@16.3.4` (2026-08-31) was adopted on 2026-09-07 after the window elapsed. `next@16.3.5` (2026-09-11) was adopted on 2026-09-18 after the window elapsed. `next@16.3.6` (2026-09-22, GHSA-vcvr-r3jv-pc5j — RCE in `next/og` `ImageResponse`) was adopted on 2026-09-23 the morning after publish. `next@16.3.8` (2026-09-30 September security train; 16.3.7 then 16.3.8) was adopted on 2026-10-02. This Linux static export with `images.unoptimized: true` does not call `next/og`; the pin still moves with the security patch. When both the announced patch and a newer 16.3.x exist, take the latest patched 16.3.x. `react@19.3.1` ships with `next@16.4.1` (2026-10-14 security train) and was adopted in Run 46. `next@16.4.2` (2026-10-15) was adopted in Run 47. `next@16.4.3` (2026-10-23 Active LTS bug-fix backport) was adopted in Run 55. `next@16.4.4` (2026-11-02 Active LTS bug-fix backport) was adopted in Run 62. `next@16.4.5` (2026-11-03 November security train — GHSA-mfr8-8qww-v76h, GHSA-vc7v-qv4w-wrv8, GHSA-vvgp-ww4w-46vf, GHSA-rhh4-g77r-r9qj, GHSA-pc58-hwjq-p385, GHSA-7qx7-33qx-rqx2, GHSA-f3mh-rxg3-f5g2) was adopted in Run 63. `next@16.4.6` (2026-11-09 Active LTS bug-fix backport — Tailwind pin in create-next-app, route-handler compression, Turbopack GC) was adopted in Run 98 with `eslint-config-next@16.4.6` and reconfirmed in Run 100. This static export with `images.unoptimized: true` does not use Cache Components, Server Actions, or the image optimizer; the pin still moves with newer 16.4.x patches. Stay on that pair unless a newer 16.4.x / 19.3.x patch ships. Canaries are at `16.5.0-canary.48`. Full `npm audit` on Run 103 still reports 8 moderate findings (eslint / typescript-eslint → `minimatch`); local `serve` stays out of that tree.
- Hosting-layer CSP on arenibus.polascin.net is inherited from the parent OpenResty site (`script-src` / `connect-src` without `formspree.io` or `'unsafe-inline'`; `form-action 'self'`). Static export cannot emit CSP headers; a subdirectory `.htaccess` would not apply (OpenResty, not Apache). Theme init and the Formspree `fetch` therefore depend on the parent CSP. The contact form keeps a `mailto:` fallback for when `fetch` is blocked. Do not add a Formspree `<form action>` and do not add a page-level meta CSP — it cannot relax an HTTP header.
- No hardcoded secrets in source. `grep` matches for `password|secret|api_key|token|private` occur only in `package-lock.json`, `.next/` build artefacts (not committed), `.audit.md` documentation, `AGENTS.md` secret-name references, and `package.json` `private: true`.
- The contact form posts to Formspree (`https://formspree.io/f/mvzebqzj`) over HTTPS. The form ID is a public endpoint identifier, not a credential.
- Static export cannot emit CSP headers. `out/index.html` carries three bare inline scripts — `themeInit` plus two Next.js RSC payload scripts — so any CSP added at the hosting layer needs hashes or `unsafe-inline` regardless of the theme script.



## 1. 11. 2026: voliteľné Clarity na verejnej prezentácii

Zavedený projekt z6sz54ayk0. Miestny načítavač sa pridá cez next/script až po hydratácii a externý Microsoft skript sa spustí iba po analytickom súhlase. Reklamný súhlas zostáva vypnutý, text stránky je maskovaný. Voľba platí 180 dní a dá sa odvolať cez Nastavenia analytiky. Zásady súkromia sú upravené; zmena sa nevzťahuje na demo ani klinickú aplikáciu.

Overené: lint, TypeScript, zostavenie Next.js 16.4.3, kontrast a odkazy. V produkcii sa zobrazila lišta, pred súhlasom nebol prítomný externý tag, po súhlase sa zaviedol správny projekt. HTTP 200, CSP povoľuje Clarity. Staršie poznámky o neúčinnosti .htaccess nezodpovedajú tejto kontrole: nasadená výsledná CSP aj správanie prehliadača boli overené priamo.

Kód po začlenení origin/main je v commite 5ad7629. Miestny scripts/deploy-reviewed.py nasadil zostavené verejné súbory z out cez alias websupport, bez mazania adresárov, so zálohami a kontrolou hashov. Posledná záloha: codex-deploy-backups/20261101T112138852738Z/arenibuspropaganda. Súhrnné merania Clarity ešte čakajú na spracovanie.

Existujúci GitHub deploy vykonáva po pushi do main zostavenie, kontrolu odkazov a kontrastu a SSH prenos out. Pri tejto zmene boli tieto kontroly vykonané miestne a použil sa miestny prenos s výslovným plánom a kontrolou súbežných zmien. Pôvodný workflow nebol zmenený ani vypnutý, push používa skip ci a skip deploy. Nový nástroj sa spúšťa ručne: --file pre vybrané verejné cesty, --plan pre uloženie hashov, --apply pre nasadenie nezmeneného plánu. Výstupom je plán a cesta overenej zálohy. Staré verzie assetov zámerne nemaže, takže nemení dostupnosť už otvorených klientov.

Poučenie: pred nasadením zosúladiť origin/main, overovať skutočnú verziu nainštalovaného Next.js a publikovať assety pred HTML, ktoré na ne odkazuje.
